Method code:
347 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
348 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
349 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
350 |       return position;
351 |     } else {
352 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.
353 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
354 |     }
355 |   }

===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSDocString(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 280-304
Documentation:

Method code:
280 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
281 |     switch (c) {
282 |       case '@':
283 |       case '*':
284 |       case ',':
285 |       case '>':
286 |       case ':':
287 |       case '(':
288 |       case ')':
289 |       case '{':
290 |       case '}':
291 |       case '[':
292 |       case ']':
293 |       case '?':
294 |       case '!':
295 |       case '|':
296 |       case '=':
297 |       case EOF_CHAR:
298 |       case '\n':
299 |         return false;
300 | 
301 |       default:
302 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
303 |     }
304 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1778-1792
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
1778 |   private static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
1779 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1780 |     int parentType = parent.getType();
1781 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
1782 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
1783 |       return n.getFirstChild();
1784 |     } else if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
1785 |       return parent;
1786 |     } else if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
1787 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
1788 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
1789 |       return parent;
1790 |     }
1791 |     return null;
1792 |   }

===== M0023 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

/**
     * Declares all of a function's arguments.
     */
Method code:
1734 |     private void declareArguments(Node functionNode) {
1735 |       Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
1736 |       Node body = astParameters.getNext();
1737 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) functionNode.getJSType();
1738 |       if (functionType != null) {
1739 |         Node jsDocParameters = functionType.getParametersNode();
1740 |         if (jsDocParameters != null) {
1741 |           Node jsDocParameter = jsDocParameters.getFirstChild();
1742 |           for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
1743 |             if (jsDocParameter != null) {
1744 |               defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode,
1745 |                   jsDocParameter.getJSType(), true);
1746 |               jsDocParameter = jsDocParameter.getNext();
1747 |             } else {
1748 |               defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode, null, true);
1749 |             }
1750 |           }
1751 |         }
1752 |       }
1753 |     } // end declareArguments

Method code:
1011 |     @Override
1012 |     /**
1013 |      * Returns a node comparator based on the pre-order traversal of the AST.
1014 |      * @param isForward x 'before' y in the pre-order traversal implies
1015 |      * x 'less than' y (if true) and x 'greater than' y (if false).
1016 |      */
1017 |     public Comparator<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> getOptionalNodeComparator(
1018 |         boolean isForward) {
1019 |       if (isForward) {
1020 |         return new Comparator<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>() {
1021 |           @Override
1022 |           public int compare(
1023 |               DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n1, DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n2) {
1024 |             return getPosition(n1) - getPosition(n2);
1025 |           }
1026 |         };
1027 |       } else {
1028 |         return new Comparator<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>() {
1029 |           @Override
1030 |           public int compare(
1031 |               DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n1, DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n2) {
1032 |             return getPosition(n2) - getPosition(n1);
1033 |           }
1034 |         };
1035 |       }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.replace()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 875-938
Documentation:
/**
     * Replace the provide statement.
     *
     * If we're providing a name with no definition, then create one.
     * If we're providing a name with a duplicate definition, then make sure
     * that definition becomes a declaration.
     */
Method code:
875 |     void replace() {
876 |       if (firstNode == null) {
877 |         // Don't touch the base case ('goog').
878 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
879 |         return;
880 |       }
881 | 
882 |       // Handle the case where there is a duplicate definition for an explicitly
883 |       // provided symbol.
884 |       if (candidateDefinition != null && explicitNode != null) {
885 |         explicitNode.detachFromParent();
886 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
887 | 
888 |         // Does this need a VAR keyword?
889 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
890 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(candidateDefinition) &&
891 |             !candidateDefinition.getFirstChild().isQualifiedName()) {
892 |           candidateDefinition.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE, true);
893 |           Node assignNode = candidateDefinition.getFirstChild();
894 |           Node nameNode = assignNode.getFirstChild();
895 |           if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
896 |             // Need to convert this assign to a var declaration.
897 |             Node valueNode = nameNode.getNext();
898 |             assignNode.removeChild(nameNode);
899 |             assignNode.removeChild(valueNode);
900 |             nameNode.addChildToFront(valueNode);
901 |             Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
902 |             varNode.copyInformationFrom(candidateDefinition);
903 |             candidateDefinition.getParent().replaceChild(
904 |                 candidateDefinition, varNode);
905 |             nameNode.setJSDocInfo(assignNode.getJSDocInfo());
906 |             compiler.reportCodeChange();
907 |             replacementNode = varNode;
908 |           }
909 |         }
910 |       } else {
911 |         // Handle the case where there's not a duplicate definition.
912 |         replacementNode = createDeclarationNode();
913 |         if (firstModule == minimumModule) {
914 |           firstNode.getParent().addChildBefore(replacementNode, firstNode);
915 |         } else {
916 |           // In this case, the name was implicitly provided by two independent
917 |           // modules. We need to move this code up to a common module.
918 |           int indexOfDot = namespace.lastIndexOf('.');
919 |           if (indexOfDot == -1) {
920 |             // Any old place is fine.
921 |             compiler.getNodeForCodeInsertion(minimumModule)
922 |                 .addChildToBack(replacementNode);
923 |           } else {
924 |             // Add it after the parent namespace.
925 |             ProvidedName parentName =
926 |                 providedNames.get(namespace.substring(0, indexOfDot));
927 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName);
928 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName.replacementNode);
929 |             parentName.replacementNode.getParent().addChildAfter(
930 |                 replacementNode, parentName.replacementNode);
931 |           }
932 |         }
933 |         if (explicitNode != null) {
934 |           explicitNode.detachFromParent();
935 |         }
936 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
937 |       }
938 |     }

===== M0038 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1717-1750
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1717 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1718 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1719 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1720 |     }
1721 | 
1722 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1723 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1724 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1725 |       skipEOLs();
1726 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1727 |         next();
1728 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1729 |       }
1730 |     }
1731 | 
1732 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName);
1733 | 
1734 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1735 |       next();
1736 |       skipEOLs();
1737 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1738 |       if (memberType != null) {
1739 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1740 | 
1741 |         skipEOLs();
1742 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1743 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1744 |         }
1745 | 
1746 |         next();
1747 |       }
1748 |     }
1749 |     return typeNameNode;
1750 |   }

555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
273 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     trace.startEvent(this);
277 |   }

126 |       .put("isDef", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
127 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
128 |           if (p.outcome) {
129 |             return getRestrictedWithoutUndefined(p.type);
130 |           } else {
131 |             return null;
132 |           }
133 |          }
134 |       })
135 |       .put("isNull", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
136 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
137 |           if (p.outcome) {
138 |             return getNativeType(NULL_TYPE);

145 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {

148 |                 getRestrictedWithoutNull(p.type));
149 |           } else {
150 |             return null;
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |       })
154 |       .put("isString", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
155 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
156 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "string", p.outcome);
157 |         }
158 |       })
159 |       .put("isBoolean", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
160 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
161 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "boolean", p.outcome);
162 |         }
163 |       })
164 |       .put("isNumber", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
165 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
166 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "number", p.outcome);
167 |         }
168 |       })
169 |       .put("isFunction", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
170 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
171 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "function", p.outcome);
172 |         }
173 |       })
174 |       .put("isArray", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
175 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
176 |           if (p.type == null) {
177 |             return p.outcome ? getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE) : null;
178 |           }
179 | 
180 |           Visitor<JSType> visitor = p.outcome ? restrictToArrayVisitor :
181 |               restrictToNotArrayVisitor;
182 |           return p.type.visit(visitor);
183 |         }
184 |       })
185 |       .put("isObject", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
186 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
187 |           if (p.type == null) {
188 |             return p.outcome ? getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE) : null;
189 |           }
190 | 
191 |           Visitor<JSType> visitor = p.outcome ? restrictToObjectVisitor :

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 506-562
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
506 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
507 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
508 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
509 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ||
510 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
511 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
512 |       if (info == null) {
513 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
514 |       }
515 |       allowDupe =
516 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
517 |     }
518 | 
519 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
520 | 
521 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
522 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
523 |     // compilation process.
524 |     if (varType != null &&
525 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
526 |         newType != null &&
527 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
528 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
529 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
530 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
531 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
532 |       // native type.
533 |       if (var.input == null) {
534 |         n.setJSType(varType);
535 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
536 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
537 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
538 |           }
539 |         } else {
540 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
541 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
542 |         }
543 |       } else {
544 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
545 |         // match the original type.
546 |         //
547 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
548 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
549 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
550 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
551 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
552 |           if (shouldReport) {
553 |             compiler.report(
554 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
555 |                     variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
556 |                     String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
557 |                     varType.toString()));
558 |           }
559 |         }
560 |       }
561 |     }
562 |   }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 169-260
Documentation:

Method code:
169 |   @Override
170 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
171 |     switch (n.getType()) {
172 |       case Token.CALL:
173 |         boolean isExpr = parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT;
174 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
175 |         if (left.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
176 |           Node name = left.getFirstChild();
177 |           if (name.getType() == Token.NAME &&
178 |               GOOG.equals(name.getString())) {
179 |             // For the sake of simplicity, we report code changes
180 |             // when we see a provides/requires, and don't worry about
181 |             // reporting the change when we actually do the replacement.
182 |             String methodName = name.getNext().getString();
183 |             if ("base".equals(methodName)) {
184 |               processBaseClassCall(t, n);
185 |             } else if (!isExpr) {
186 |               // All other methods must be called in an EXPR.
187 |               break;
188 |             } else if ("require".equals(methodName)) {
189 |               processRequireCall(t, n, parent);
190 |             } else if ("provide".equals(methodName)) {
191 |               processProvideCall(t, n, parent);
192 |             } else if ("exportSymbol".equals(methodName)) {
193 |               Node arg = left.getNext();
194 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.STRING) {
195 |                 int dot = arg.getString().indexOf('.');
196 |                 if (dot == -1) {
197 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString());
198 |                 } else {
199 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString().substring(0, dot));
200 |                 }
201 |               }
202 |             } else if ("addDependency".equals(methodName)) {
203 |               CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
204 |               List<String> typeDecls =
205 |                   convention.identifyTypeDeclarationCall(n);
206 |               if (typeDecls != null) {
207 |                 for (String typeDecl : typeDecls) {
208 |                   compiler.getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(typeDecl);
209 |                 }
210 |               }
211 | 
212 |               // We can't modify parent, so just create a node that will
213 |               // get compiled out.
214 |               parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(0));
215 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
216 |             } else if ("setCssNameMapping".equals(methodName)) {
217 |               processSetCssNameMapping(t, n, parent);
218 |             }
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |         break;
222 | 
223 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
224 |       case Token.NAME:
225 |         // If this is an assignment to a provided name, remove the provided
226 |         // object.
227 |         handleCandidateProvideDefinition(t, n, parent);
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
231 |         handleTypedefDefinition(t, n, parent);
232 |         break;
233 | 
234 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
235 |         // If this is a declaration of a provided named function, this is an
236 |         // error. Hosited functions will explode if the're provided.
237 |         if (t.inGlobalScope() &&
238 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
239 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
240 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
241 |           if (pn != null) {
242 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, FUNCTION_NAMESPACE_ERROR, name));
243 |           }
244 |         }
245 |         break;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         trySimplifyNewDate(t, n, parent);
249 |         break;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.GETPROP:
252 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME &&
253 |             parent.getType() != Token.CALL &&
254 |             parent.getType() != Token.ASSIGN &&
255 |             "goog.base".equals(n.getQualifiedName())) {
256 |           reportBadBaseClassUse(t, n, "May only be called directly.");
257 |         }
258 |         break;
259 |     }
260 |   }

===== M0083 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1538-1581
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1538 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1539 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1540 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1541 | 
1542 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1543 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1544 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1545 |       return;
1546 |     }
1547 | 
1548 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1549 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1550 |     if (childType instanceof FunctionType) {
1551 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) childType;
1552 | 
1553 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1554 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1555 |       if(functionJSDocInfo != null) {
1556 |         String sourceName = functionJSDocInfo.getSourceName();
1557 |         CompilerInput functionSource = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
1558 |         isExtern = functionSource.isExtern();
1559 |       }
1560 | 
1561 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1562 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1563 |       // in an extern.
1564 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1565 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1566 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1567 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1568 |            !isExtern)) {
1569 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1570 |       }
1571 | 
1572 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1573 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1574 |     } else {
1575 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1576 |     }
1577 | 
1578 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1579 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1580 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.

===== M0097 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0107 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 129-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
129 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
130 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
131 |     String methodName = null;
132 |     if (callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
133 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
134 |     } else if (callName.getType() == Token.NAME) {
135 |       String name = callName.getString();
136 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
137 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
138 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
139 |       }
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     if (methodName != null) {
143 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
144 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
145 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
146 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
147 |       }
148 |     }
149 |     return null;
150 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(NodeTraversal,Node)

Documentation:

Method code:
305 |   @Override
306 |   public ObjectLiteralCast getObjectLiteralCast(NodeTraversal t,
307 |       Node callNode) {
308 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(callNode.getType() == Token.CALL);
309 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
310 |     if (!"goog.reflect.object".equals(callName.getQualifiedName()) ||
311 |         callName.getChildCount() != 2) {

===== M0140 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1865-1889
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1865 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1866 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1867 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1868 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1869 |             type.isUnknownType());
1870 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1871 |     if (info != null) {
1872 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1873 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1874 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1875 |         type = infoType;
1876 |       }
1877 | 
1878 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1879 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1880 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1881 |         compiler.report(
1882 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1883 |       }
1884 |     }
1885 | 
1886 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1887 |       n.setJSType(type);
1888 |     }
1889 |   }

===== M0149 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 832-874
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
832 |   @Override
833 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
834 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
835 |       return true;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
839 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
840 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
841 |         return true;
842 |       }
843 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
844 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
845 |         return false;
846 |       }
847 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
848 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
849 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
850 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
851 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
852 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
853 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
854 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
855 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
856 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
857 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
858 | 
859 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
860 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
861 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
862 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
863 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
864 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
865 | 
866 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
867 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
868 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
869 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
870 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
874 |   }

===== M0150 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 467-487
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
478 |             unknown = true;
479 |             break;
480 |           }
481 |         }
482 |       } else {
483 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
484 |       }
485 |     }
486 |     return unknown;
487 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java

51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT

64 |         // a bad idea.

79 |               if (newLevel != null) {
80 |                 return newLevel;
81 |               }
82 |             }
83 |           }
84 |         }
85 |       }
86 |     }
87 |     return null;
88 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.allFlowSlots()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the symbols that have been defined before this point
   * in the current flow. Does not return slots that have not changed during
   * the flow.
   *
   * For example, consider the code:
   * <code>
   * var x = 3;
   * function f() {
   *   var y = 5;
   *   y = 6; // FLOW POINT
   *   var z = y;
   *   return z;
   * }
   * </code>
   * A FlowScope at FLOW POINT will return a slot for y, but not
   * a slot for x or z.
   */
Method code:
340 |   private Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> allFlowSlots() {
341 |     Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slots = Maps.newHashMap();
342 |     for (LinkedFlowSlot slot = lastSlot;
343 |          slot != null; slot = slot.parent) {
344 |       if (!slots.containsKey(slot.getName())) {
345 |         slots.put(slot.getName(), slot);
346 |       }
347 |     }
348 | 
349 |     for (String key : cache.symbols.keySet()) {
350 |       if (!slots.containsKey(key)) {
351 |         slots.put(key, cache.symbols.get(key));
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     return slots;
356 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0172 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0175 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 118-257
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
119 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
120 |     // Check for the typeof operator.
121 |     int operatorToken = condition.getType();
122 |     switch (operatorToken) {
123 |       case Token.EQ:
124 |       case Token.NE:
125 |       case Token.SHEQ:
126 |       case Token.SHNE:
127 |       case Token.CASE:
128 |         Node left;
129 |         Node right;
130 |         if (operatorToken == Token.CASE) {
131 |           left = condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
132 |           right = condition.getFirstChild();
133 |         } else {
134 |           left = condition.getFirstChild();
135 |           right = condition.getLastChild();
136 |         }
137 | 
138 |         Node typeOfNode = null;
139 |         Node stringNode = null;
140 |         if (left.getType() == Token.TYPEOF && right.getType() == Token.STRING) {
141 |           typeOfNode = left;
142 |           stringNode = right;
143 |         } else if (right.getType() == Token.TYPEOF &&
144 |                    left.getType() == Token.STRING) {
145 |           typeOfNode = right;
146 |           stringNode = left;
147 |         }
148 |         if (typeOfNode != null && stringNode != null) {
149 |           Node operandNode = typeOfNode.getFirstChild();
150 |           JSType operandType = getTypeIfRefinable(operandNode, blindScope);
151 |           if (operandType != null) {
152 |             boolean resultEqualsValue = operatorToken == Token.EQ ||
153 |                 operatorToken == Token.SHEQ || operatorToken == Token.CASE;
154 |             if (!outcome) {
155 |               resultEqualsValue = !resultEqualsValue;
156 |             }
157 |             return caseTypeOf(operandNode, operandType, stringNode.getString(),
158 |                 resultEqualsValue, blindScope);
159 |           }
160 |         }
161 |     }
162 |     switch (operatorToken) {
163 |       case Token.AND:
164 |         if (outcome) {
165 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
166 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
167 |         } else {
168 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
169 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
170 |         }
171 | 
172 |       case Token.OR:
173 |         if (!outcome) {
174 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
175 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
176 |         } else {
177 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
178 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
179 |         }
180 | 
181 |       case Token.EQ:
182 |         if (outcome) {
183 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
184 |         } else {
185 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
186 |         }
187 | 
188 |       case Token.NE:
189 |         if (outcome) {
190 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
191 |         } else {
192 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
193 |         }
194 | 
195 |       case Token.SHEQ:
196 |         if (outcome) {
197 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
198 |         } else {
199 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
200 |         }
201 | 
202 |       case Token.SHNE:
203 |         if (outcome) {
204 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
205 |         } else {
206 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
207 |         }
208 | 
209 |       case Token.NAME:
210 |       case Token.GETPROP:
211 |         return caseNameOrGetProp(condition, blindScope, outcome);
212 | 
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
214 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
215 |             condition.getFirstChild(),
216 |             firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
217 |                 condition.getFirstChild().getNext(), blindScope, outcome),
218 |             outcome);
219 | 
220 |       case Token.NOT:
221 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
222 |             condition.getFirstChild(), blindScope, !outcome);
223 | 
224 |       case Token.LE:
225 |       case Token.LT:
226 |       case Token.GE:
227 |       case Token.GT:
228 |         if (outcome) {
229 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, INEQ);
230 |         }
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
234 |         return caseInstanceOf(
235 |             condition.getFirstChild(), condition.getLastChild(), blindScope,
236 |             outcome);
237 | 
238 |       case Token.IN:
239 |         if (outcome && condition.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.STRING) {
240 |           return caseIn(condition.getLastChild(),
241 |               condition.getFirstChild().getString(), blindScope);
242 |         }
243 |         break;
244 | 
245 |       case Token.CASE:
246 |         Node left =
247 |             condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
248 |         Node right = condition.getFirstChild();
249 |         if (outcome) {
250 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHEQ);
251 |         } else {
252 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHNE);
253 |         }
254 |     }
255 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
256 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);
257 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType,Node,String)

Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
340 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
341 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
342 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.
343 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
344 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
345 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
346 |         // the line numbers of both types.
347 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType);
348 |       } else {
349 |         mismatch(t, n,
350 |             "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
351 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
352 |             rightType, leftType);
353 |       }
354 |       return false;
355 |     }
356 |     return true;
357 |   }

===== M0190 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1467-1529
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1467 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1468 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1469 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1470 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1471 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1472 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1473 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1474 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1475 |           (baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1476 |         compiler.report(
1477 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1478 |       } else {
1479 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1480 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1481 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1482 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1483 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1484 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1485 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1486 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1487 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1488 |               badImplementedType = true;
1489 |             }
1490 |           } else {
1491 |             badImplementedType = true;
1492 |           }
1493 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1494 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1495 |           }
1496 |         }
1497 |         // check properties
1498 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1499 |       }
1500 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1501 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1502 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1503 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1504 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1505 |           compiler.report(
1506 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1507 |         }
1508 |       }
1509 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1510 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1511 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1512 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1513 |       }
1514 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1515 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1516 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1517 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1518 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1519 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1520 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1521 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1522 |           currentProperties.clear();
1523 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1524 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1525 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1526 |         }
1527 |       }
1528 |     }
1529 |   }

===== M0205 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 632-671
Documentation:

Method code:
632 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
633 |     String varName = n.getString();
634 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
635 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
636 |     if (value != null) {
637 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
638 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
639 |           getJSType(value));
640 |       return scope;
641 |     } else {
642 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
643 |       if (var != null) {
644 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
645 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
646 | 
647 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
648 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
649 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
650 |         boolean unflowable =
651 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
652 | 
653 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
654 |         // inferred variable.
655 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
656 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
657 |             isInferred &&
658 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
659 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
660 | 
661 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
662 |           type = var.getType();
663 |           if (type == null) {
664 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
665 |           }
666 |         }
667 |       }
668 |     }
669 |     n.setJSType(type);
670 |     return scope;
671 |   }

/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of

===== M0210 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 69-122
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
71 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
72 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
73 |     if (type != null) {
74 |       Node subclass = null;
75 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
76 | 
77 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
78 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
79 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
80 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
81 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
82 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
83 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
84 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
85 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP;
86 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
87 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
88 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
89 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
90 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
91 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
95 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
96 |         // inheritance.
97 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
98 |           return null;
99 |         }
100 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
101 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
102 |             return null;
103 |           }
104 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
105 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
106 |         }
107 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
108 |       }
109 | 
110 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
111 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
112 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
113 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
114 |       if (subclass != null &&
115 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
116 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
117 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
118 |       }
119 |     }
120 | 
121 |     return null;
122 |   }

===== M0212 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1586-1620
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1586 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1587 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1588 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1589 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1590 | 
1591 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1592 |     int ordinal = 0;
1593 |     Node parameter = null;
1594 |     Node argument = null;
1595 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1596 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1597 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1598 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1599 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1600 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1601 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1602 |       }
1603 |       argument = arguments.next();
1604 |       ordinal++;
1605 | 
1606 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1607 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1608 |     }
1609 | 
1610 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1611 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1612 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1613 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1614 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1615 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1616 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1617 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1618 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1619 |     }

/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
581 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
582 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
583 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
584 |       return that.getLeastSupertype(this);
585 |     }
586 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
587 |   }

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1156-1204
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1156 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1157 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1158 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1159 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1160 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1161 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1162 |               (FunctionType) rValue.getJSType(), info, lValue)) {
1163 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1164 |       } else if (info != null) {
1165 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1166 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1167 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1168 |           } else {
1169 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1170 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1171 |           }
1172 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1173 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1174 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1175 |         } else {
1176 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1177 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1178 |             JSType knownType = null;
1179 |             if (rValue != null) {
1180 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1181 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1182 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1183 |               } else if (rValue.getType() == Token.OR) {
1184 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1185 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1186 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1187 |                 // reasons.
1188 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1189 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1190 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.getType() == Token.NAME
1191 |                     && lValue.getType() == Token.NAME
1192 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1193 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1194 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1195 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1196 |                 }
1197 |               }
1198 |             }
1199 |           }
1200 |         }
1201 |       }
1202 | 
1203 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1204 |     }

===== M0225 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 582-639
Documentation:

Method code:
582 |     @Override
583 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
584 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
585 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
586 |       if (name == null) {
587 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
588 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
589 |           errorReporter.error(
590 |             "unnamed function statement",
591 |             sourceName,
592 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
593 |         }
594 |         name = new Name();
595 |         name.setIdentifier("");
596 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
597 |       }
598 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
599 |       Node newName = transform(name);
600 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
601 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
602 |         // declaration.
603 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
604 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
605 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
606 |         // same line as the function keyword.
607 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
608 |             functionNode.getLp();
609 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
610 |       }
611 | 
612 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
613 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
614 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
615 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
616 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
617 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
618 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
619 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
620 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
621 |       if (fnName != null) {
622 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
623 |       } else {
624 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
625 |       }
626 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
627 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
628 | 
629 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
630 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
631 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
632 |       }
633 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
634 | 
635 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
636 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
637 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
638 |      return node;
639 |     }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 654-680
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infimums, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
654 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
655 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
656 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
657 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
658 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
659 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
660 |       UnionType unionType = (UnionType) type;
661 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
662 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
663 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
664 |           needsFiltering = true;
665 |           break;
666 |         }
667 |       }
668 | 
669 |       if (needsFiltering) {
670 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
671 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
672 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
673 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
674 |           }
675 |         }
676 |         return builder.build();
677 |       }
678 |     }
679 |     return type;
680 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:

1096 | 
1097 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1098 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1099 | 
1100 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
1101 |         true);
1102 |     return typeNode;
1103 |   }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1162-1170
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1162 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1163 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1164 |       return -1;
1165 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1166 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1167 |     } else {
1168 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1169 |     }
1170 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1752-1766
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1752 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1753 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1754 |       return getString();
1755 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1756 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1757 |       if (left == null) {
1758 |         return null;
1759 |       }
1760 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1761 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1762 |       return "this";
1763 |     } else {
1764 |       return null;
1765 |     }
1766 |   }

===== M0255 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 180-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
180 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
181 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
182 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
183 |     // show up in the type registry.
184 |     Scope newScope = null;
185 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
186 |     if (parent == null) {
187 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
188 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
189 | 
190 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
191 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
192 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
193 |     } else {
194 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
195 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
196 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
197 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
198 |     }
199 | 
200 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
201 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
202 | 
203 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
204 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
205 |     // build properties on.
206 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
207 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
208 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
209 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
210 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
211 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
212 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
213 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
214 |         }
215 |       }
216 |     }
217 | 
218 |     if (parent == null) {
219 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
220 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
221 |     }
222 |     return newScope;
223 |   }

/**
   * Handle cases #1 and #3 in the class doc.
   */
Method code:
190 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
191 |       ObjectType objType, JSDocInfo docInfo, @Nullable String qName) {
192 |     if (objType.isConstructor() ||
193 |         objType.isEnumType() ||
194 |         objType.isInterface()) {
195 |       // Named types.
196 |       if (objType.hasReferenceName() &&
197 |           objType.getReferenceName().equals(qName)) {
198 |         objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
199 | 
200 |         if (objType.isConstructor() || objType.isInterface()) {
201 |           ((FunctionType) objType).getInstanceType().setJSDocInfo(
202 |               docInfo);
203 |         } else if (objType instanceof EnumType) {
204 |           ((EnumType) objType).getElementsType().setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
205 |         }
206 |       }
207 |     } else if (!objType.isNativeObjectType() &&
208 |         objType.isFunctionType()) {
209 |       // Structural functions.
210 |       objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
211 |     }
212 |   }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1591 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1592 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1593 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1594 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1595 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1596 |         next();
1597 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1598 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1599 |           next();
1600 |         }
1601 |         skipEOLs();
1602 |         token = next();
1603 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1604 |       }
1605 |     }
1606 |     return typeExpr;
1607 |   }
