===== M0276 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 169-913
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
169 |   boolean parse() {
170 |     int lineno;
171 |     int charno;
172 | 
173 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
174 |     JSTypeExpression type;
175 | 
176 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
177 |     skipEOLs();
178 | 
179 |     JsDocToken token = next();
180 | 
181 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
182 | 
183 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
184 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
185 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
186 |       token = blockInfo.token;
187 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
188 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
189 |       }
190 |     } else {
191 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
192 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
193 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
194 |         // what it was.
195 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
196 |       }
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
200 |     retry: for (;;) {
201 |       switch (token) {
202 |         case ANNOTATION:
203 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
204 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
205 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
206 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
207 | 
208 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
209 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
210 |             if (annotation == null) {
211 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
212 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
213 |             } else {
214 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
215 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
216 | 
217 |               switch (annotation) {
218 |                 case AUTHOR:
219 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
220 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
221 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
222 | 
223 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
224 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
225 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                     } else {
227 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
228 |                     }
229 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
230 |                   } else {
231 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
232 |                   }
233 |                   continue retry;
234 | 
235 |                 case CONSTANT:
236 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
237 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
238 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                   }
240 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
241 |                   continue retry;
242 | 
243 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
244 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
245 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
246 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
247 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
248 |                     } else {
249 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
250 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                     }
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case DEPRECATED:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }
261 | 
262 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
263 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
264 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
265 | 
266 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
267 |

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)

/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1640 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1641 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1642 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1643 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1644 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1645 |       // {?} - right curly
1646 |       // {?=} - equals
1647 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1648 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1649 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1650 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1651 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1652 |       token = next();
1653 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1654 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1655 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1656 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1657 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1658 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1659 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1660 |       }
1661 | 
1662 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1663 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1664 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1665 |     } else {
1666 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1667 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1668 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1669 |           next();
1670 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1671 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1672 |           next();
1673 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1674 |         }
1675 |       }
1676 | 
1677 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1678 |     }
1679 |   }

Documentation:
/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
228 |   public int getMinArguments() {
229 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
230 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
231 |     // of the last required parameter.
232 |     int i = 0;
233 |     int min = 0;
234 |     for (Node n : getParameters()) {
235 |       i++;
236 |       if (!n.isOptionalArg() && !n.isVarArgs()) {
237 |         min = i;
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |     return min;
241 |   }

===== M0285 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1213-1242
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1213 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1214 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1215 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1216 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1217 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1218 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1219 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1220 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1221 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1222 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1223 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1224 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1225 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1226 |       return false;
1227 |     }
1228 | 
1229 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1230 |     if (type == null) {
1231 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1232 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1233 |       if (var != null) {
1234 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1235 |         if (varType != null) {
1236 |           type = varType;
1237 |         }
1238 |       }
1239 |     }
1240 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1241 |     return true;
1242 |   }

===== M0291 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1658-1677
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1658 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1659 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1660 |       case Token.IF:
1661 |       case Token.WHILE:
1662 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1663 |       case Token.DO:
1664 |         return n.getLastChild();
1665 |       case Token.FOR:
1666 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
1667 |           case 3:
1668 |             return null;
1669 |           case 4:
1670 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1671 |         }
1672 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1673 |       case Token.CASE:
1674 |         return null;
1675 |     }
1676 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1677 |   }

===== M0292 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 774-873
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
774 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
775 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
776 |         @Nullable String name,
777 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
778 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
779 | 
780 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
781 | 
782 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
783 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
784 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
785 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
786 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = (FunctionType) var.getType();
787 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
788 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
789 |             functionType = aliasedType;
790 | 
791 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
792 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
793 |             }
794 |           }
795 |         }
796 |       }
797 | 
798 |       if (functionType == null) {
799 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
800 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
801 |             rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
802 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
803 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
804 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
805 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
806 | 
807 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
808 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
809 | 
810 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
811 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
812 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
813 |             functionType = (FunctionType) type;
814 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
815 |           }
816 |         }
817 | 
818 |         if (functionType == null) {
819 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
820 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
821 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
822 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
823 |               lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
824 |             Var var = scope.getVar(
825 |                 lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
826 |             if (var != null) {
827 |               ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
828 |               if (ownerType != null) {
829 |                 String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
830 |                 overriddenPropType =
831 |                     findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
832 |               }
833 |             }
834 |           }
835 | 
836 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
837 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
838 |                   scope)
839 |               .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
840 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
841 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
842 |               .inferReturnType(info)
843 |               .inferInheritance(info);
844 | 
845 |           // Infer the context type.
846 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
847 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
848 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
849 |             Node objNode = lvalueNode.getFirstChild();
850 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
851 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
852 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
853 |               searchedForThisType = true;
854 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
855 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
856 |               searchedForThisType = true;
857 |             }
858 |           }
859 | 
860 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
861 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
862 |           }
863 | 
864 |           functionType = builder
865 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
866 |               .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
867 |               .buildAndRegister();
868 |         }
869 |       }
870 | 
871 |       // all done
872 |       return functionType;
873 |     }

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 310-317
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

   *

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 835-940
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
835 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
836 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
837 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
838 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
839 | 
840 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
841 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
842 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
843 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
844 | 
845 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
846 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
847 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
848 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
849 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
850 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
851 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
852 |         }
853 |       }
854 | 
855 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
856 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
857 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
858 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
859 |             property, rvalue);
860 |         return;
861 |       }
862 | 
863 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
864 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
865 |         checkEnumInitializer(
866 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
867 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
868 |         return;
869 |       }
870 | 
871 |       // object.prototype = ...;
872 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
873 |         if (objectJsType instanceof FunctionType) {
874 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) objectJsType;
875 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
876 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
877 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
878 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
879 |           }
880 |         } else {
881 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
882 |         }
883 |         return;
884 |       }
885 | 
886 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
887 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
888 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
889 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
890 | 
891 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
892 |           JSType jsType = object2.getJSType();
893 |           if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
894 |             FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
895 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
896 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
897 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
898 |             }
899 |           } else {
900 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
901 |           }
902 |           return;
903 |         }
904 |       }
905 | 
906 |       // object.property = ...;
907 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
908 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
909 |       if (type != null) {
910 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
911 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
912 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
913 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
914 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
915 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
916 |         }
917 |         return;
918 |       }
919 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
920 |       // variable with inferred type case
921 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
922 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
923 |       if (var != null) {
924 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
925 |           return;
926 |         }
927 |       }
928 |     }
929 | 
930 |     // fall through case
931 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
932 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
933 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
934 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
935 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
936 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
937 |     } else {
938 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
939 |     }
940 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 417-443
Documentation:

Method code:
417 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
418 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
419 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
420 |     switch (n.getType()) {
421 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
422 |         // normal type checking
423 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
424 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
425 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
426 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
427 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
428 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
429 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
430 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
431 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
432 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
433 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
434 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
435 |         }
436 | 
437 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
438 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
439 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
440 |         break;
441 |     }
442 |     return true;
443 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 313-343
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
313 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
314 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
315 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
316 |       return this;
317 |     }
318 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
319 |     if (returnType == null) {
320 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
321 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
322 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
323 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
324 |         int cType = current.getType();
325 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null) {
326 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
327 |           break;
328 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
329 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
330 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
331 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
332 |             worklist.add(child);
333 |           }
334 |         }
335 |       }
336 | 
337 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
338 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
339 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
340 |       }
341 |     }
342 |     return this;
343 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1362-1395
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1362 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1363 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1364 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1365 |     // merge to fix this.
1366 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1367 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1368 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1369 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1370 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1371 | 
1372 |       if (value != null) {
1373 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1374 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1375 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1376 | 
1377 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1378 |         if (info == null) {
1379 |           info = varInfo;
1380 |         }
1381 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1382 |           // var.getType() can never be null, this would indicate a bug in the
1383 |           // scope creation logic.
1384 |           checkEnumInitializer(
1385 |               t, value,
1386 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
1387 |         } else if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1388 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1389 |         } else {
1390 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1391 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1392 |         }
1393 |       }
1394 |     }
1395 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

===== M0401 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 457-477
Documentation:

Method code:
457 |     @Override
458 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
459 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
460 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> predNodes =
461 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);
462 |       List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(predNodes.size());
463 | 
464 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> predNode : predNodes) {
465 |         BranchedFlowState<L> predNodeState = predNode.getAnnotation();
466 |

468 |             getCfg().getDirectedSuccNodes(predNode).indexOf(node));
469 | 
470 |         values.add(in);
471 |       }
472 |       if (getCfg().getEntry() == node) {
473 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
474 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
475 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
476 |       }

/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1365 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1366 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {

1375 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1376 | 
1377 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1378 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1379 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1380 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1381 |         }
1382 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1383 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1384 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1385 | 
1386 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1387 |         return createOptionalType(
1388 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1389 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1390 | 
1391 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1392 |         return createOptionalType(
1393 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1394 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1395 | 
1396 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1397 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1398 | 
1399 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1400 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1401 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1402 | 
1403 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1404 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1405 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1406 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1407 |           builder.addAlternate(
1408 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1409 |         }
1410 |         return builder.build();
1411 | 
1412 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1413 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1414 | 
1415 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1416 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1417 | 
1418 |       case Token.STRING:
1419 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1420 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1421 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1422 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1423 |         }
1424 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1425 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1426 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1427 |           if (typeList != null &&
1428 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1429 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1430 |             JSType parameterType =
1431 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1432 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1433 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1434 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1435 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1436 |               JSType indexType =
1437 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1438 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1439 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1440 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1441 |             }
1442 |           }
1443 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1444 |         } else {
1445 |           return namedType;
1446 |         }
1447 | 
1448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1449 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1450 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1451 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1452 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1453 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1454 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1455 |           thisType =
1456 |               ObjectType.cast(
1457 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1458 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1459 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1460 |           if (thisType == null) {
1461 |             reporter.warning(
1462 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1463 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1464 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1465 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1466 |                 sourceName,
1467 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), "", contextNode.getCharno());
1468 |           }
1469 | 
1470 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1471 |           current = current.getNext();
1472 |         }
1473 | 
1474 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1475 | 
1476 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1477 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1478 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1479 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1480 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1481 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1482 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1483 |               } else {
1484 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1485 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1486 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1487 |               }
1488 |             } else {
1489 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1490 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1491 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1492 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1493 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1494 |                   reporter.warning(
1495 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1496 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1497 |                 }
1498 |               } else {
1499 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1500 |               }
1501 |             }
1502 |           }
1503 |           current = current.getNext();
1504 |         }
1505 | 
1506 |         JSType returnType =
1507 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1508 | 
1509 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1510 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1511 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1512 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1513 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1514 |             .build();
1515 |     }
1516 | 
1517 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1518 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1519 |   }

413 |     @Override
414 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n,
415 |         Node parent) {
416 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
417 |           n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
418 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
419 |       }
420 | 
421 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
422 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
423 |       boolean descend = parent == null || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
424 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
425 | 
426 |       if (descend) {
427 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
428 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
429 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
430 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
431 |                child != null;
432 |                child = child.getNext()) {
433 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
434 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
435 |             }
436 |           }
437 |         }
438 |       }
439 | 
440 |       return descend;
441 |     }

===== M0407 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0424 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

===== M0432 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1020-1131
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1020 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1021 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1022 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1023 | 
1024 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.getType() == Token.NAME && scope.isGlobal();
1025 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1026 |           isGlobalVar &&
1027 |           (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
1028 |            parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
1029 | 
1030 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1031 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1032 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1033 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1034 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1035 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1036 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1037 | 
1038 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1039 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1040 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1041 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1042 |         }
1043 |       }
1044 | 
1045 |       // declared in closest scope?
1046 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1047 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1048 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1049 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1050 |       } else {
1051 |         if (!inferred) {
1052 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1053 |         }
1054 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
1055 |         boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
1056 |         Var newVar =
1057 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1058 | 
1059 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1060 |           ObjectType globalThis =
1061 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1062 |           if (inferred) {
1063 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1064 |                 type == null ?
1065 |                     getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1066 |                     type,
1067 |                 isExtern, n);
1068 |           } else {
1069 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern, n);
1070 |           }
1071 |         }
1072 | 
1073 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1074 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1075 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1076 |               (initialValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT ||
1077 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1078 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1079 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1080 |           }
1081 |         }
1082 | 
1083 |         // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1084 |         if (type instanceof FunctionType &&
1085 |             // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1086 |             !type.isEmptyType()) {
1087 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
1088 |           if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1089 |               !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1090 |             // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1091 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1092 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
1093 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), input,
1094 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1095 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
1096 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1097 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1098 | 
1099 |             // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1100 |             // it constructs itself.
1101 |             if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1102 |                 !isExtern &&
1103 |                 // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1104 |                 // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1105 |                 // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1106 |                 // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1107 |                 // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1108 |                 // this.
1109 |                 variableName.equals(
1110 |                     fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1111 |               compiler.report(
1112 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1113 |                       fnType.isConstructor() ?
1114 |                           CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1115 |                       variableName));
1116 |             }
1117 |           }
1118 |         }
1119 |       }
1120 | 
1121 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1122 |           && type instanceof FunctionType
1123 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1124 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1125 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1126 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1127 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1128 |         globalThisCtor
1129 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn(((FunctionType) type).getInstanceType());
1130 |       }
1131 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 242-268
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
242 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
243 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
244 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
245 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
246 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
247 | 
248 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
249 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
250 |       // if (this.x == null) {
251 |       //   this.initializeX();
252 |       //   this.x.foo();
253 |       // }
254 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
255 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
256 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
257 |       //
258 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
259 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
260 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
261 |         return true;
262 |       }
263 | 
264 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
265 |       return false;
266 |     }
267 |     return true;
268 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1323-1431
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1323 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1324 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1325 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1326 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1327 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1328 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1329 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1330 | 
1331 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1332 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1333 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1334 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1335 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1336 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1337 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1338 |         if (qVar != null) {
1339 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1340 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1341 |             return;
1342 |           }
1343 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1344 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1345 |           }
1346 |         }
1347 |       }
1348 | 
1349 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1350 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1351 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1352 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1353 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1354 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1355 |       //
1356 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1357 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1358 |       //
1359 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1360 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1361 | 
1362 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1363 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1364 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1365 |         // Determining type for #5
1366 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1367 |       }
1368 | 
1369 |       if (valueType == null) {
1370 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1371 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1372 |               n,
1373 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1374 |               ownerName));
1375 |         }
1376 | 
1377 |         return;
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       boolean inferred = true;
1381 |       if (info != null) {
1382 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3 + #4
1383 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1384 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1385 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1386 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1387 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1388 |       }
1389 | 
1390 |       if (inferred) {
1391 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1392 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1393 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1394 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1395 |       }
1396 | 
1397 |       if (!inferred) {
1398 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1399 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1400 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1401 |           // declared yet.
1402 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1403 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1404 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1405 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1406 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1407 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1408 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern, n);
1409 |           }
1410 |         }
1411 | 
1412 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1413 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1414 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1415 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1416 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1417 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1418 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1419 |         if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1420 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1421 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1422 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1423 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1424 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1425 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1426 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1427 |                 true);
1428 |           }
1429 |         }
1430 |       }
1431 |     }

===== M0486 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1772-1782
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns whether a node corresponds to a simple or a qualified name, such as
   * <code>x</code> or <code>a.b.c</code> or <code>this.a</code>.
   */
Method code:
1772 |   public boolean isQualifiedName() {
1773 |     switch (getType()) {
1774 |       case Token.NAME:
1775 |       case Token.THIS:
1776 |         return true;
1777 |       case Token.GETPROP:

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 730-756
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
730 |   @Override
731 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
732 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
733 |       return false;
734 |     }
735 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
736 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
737 |       return false;
738 |     }
739 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
740 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
741 |         return this == that;
742 |       }
743 |       return false;
744 |     }
745 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
747 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
748 |       }
749 |       return false;
750 |     }
751 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
755 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
756 |   }

===== M0506 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.FlatFlowScopeCache.FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 412-476
Documentation:
// A cache at the join of two scope chains.
Method code:
412 |     FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeA,
413 |         LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeB) {
414 |       linkedEquivalent = null;
415 | 
416 |       // Always prefer the "real" function scope to the faked-out
417 |       // bottom scope.
418 |       functionScope = joinedScopeA.flowsFromBottom() ?
419 |           joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope() : joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope();
420 | 
421 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsA = joinedScopeA.allFlowSlots();
422 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsB = joinedScopeB.allFlowSlots();
423 | 
424 |       symbols = slotsA;
425 | 
426 |       // There are 5 different join cases:
427 |       // 1) The type is declared in joinedScopeA, not in joinedScopeB,
428 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in A.
429 |       // 2) The type is declared in joinedScopeB, not in joinedScopeA,
430 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in B.
431 |       // 3) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeA, but
432 |       //    not in joinedScopeB. Join the two types.
433 |       // 4) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeB, but
434 |       //    not in joinedScopeA. Join the two types.
435 |       // 5) The type is declared in joinedScopeA and joinedScopeB. Join
436 |       //    the two types.
437 |       Set<String> symbolNames = Sets.newHashSet(symbols.keySet());
438 |       symbolNames.addAll(slotsB.keySet());
439 | 
440 |       for (String name : symbolNames) {
441 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotA = slotsA.get(name);
442 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotB = slotsB.get(name);
443 | 
444 |         JSType joinedType = null;
445 |         if (slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null) {
446 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
447 |               = joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
448 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
449 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
450 |             // Case #1 -- already inserted.
451 |           } else {
452 |             // Case #3
453 |             joinedType = slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
454 |           }
455 |         } else if (slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null) {
456 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
457 |               = joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
458 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
459 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
460 |             // Case #2
461 |             symbols.put(name, slotB);
462 |           } else {
463 |             // Case #4
464 |             joinedType = slotB.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
465 |           }
466 |         } else {
467 |           // Case #5
468 |           joinedType =
469 |               slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(slotB.getType());
470 |         }
471 | 
472 |         if (joinedType != null) {
473 |           symbols.put(name, new SimpleSlot(name, joinedType, true));
474 |         }
475 |       }
476 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1252 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1253 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1254 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1255 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1256 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1257 |       return;
1258 |     }
1259 | 
1260 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1261 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1262 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1263 |     // and what isn't.
1264 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1265 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1266 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1267 | 
1268 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1269 |     // non-object.
1270 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1271 |             childType + " has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1272 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1273 |       return;
1274 |     }
1275 | 
1276 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1277 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1278 |   }

962 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);

Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0524 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-276
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
236 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
237 |       options.checkTypes = true;
238 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
239 |       options.checkTypes = false;
240 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
241 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
242 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
243 |       // parser type warnings.
244 |       options.setWarningLevel(
245 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
246 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
247 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
253 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
257 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
258 |     guards.add(
259 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
260 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
261 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
262 | 
263 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
264 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
265 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
266 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
267 |     // error off.
268 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
269 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
270 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
271 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
272 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
276 |   }

     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();

245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }

491 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
492 |       switch (n.getType()) {
493 |         case Token.NULL:
494 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
495 |           break;
496 | 
497 |         case Token.VOID:
498 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
499 |           break;
500 | 
501 |         case Token.STRING:
502 |           // Defer keys to the Token.OBJECTLIT case
503 |           if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
504 |             n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
505 |           }
506 |           break;
507 | 
508 |         case Token.NUMBER:
509 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
510 |           break;
511 | 
512 |         case Token.TRUE:
513 |         case Token.FALSE:
514 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
515 |           break;
516 | 
517 |         case Token.REGEXP:
518 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
519 |           break;
520 | 
521 |         case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
522 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
523 |           break;
524 | 
525 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
526 |           defineObjectLiteral(t, n);
527 |           break;
528 | 
529 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): If we ever support Array tuples,
530 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
531 |       }
532 |     }

===== M0540 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 545-590
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
545 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
546 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
547 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
548 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
549 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
550 |     if (objectType == null) {
551 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
552 |     } else {
553 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
554 |         return;
555 |       }
556 | 
557 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
558 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
559 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
560 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
561 |         //
562 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
563 |         //    inferred type.
564 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
565 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
566 |         //    define it.
567 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
568 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
569 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
570 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
571 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
572 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
573 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
574 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
575 |           } else {
576 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
577 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
578 |           }
579 |         } else {
580 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
581 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
582 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
583 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
584 |           } else {
585 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
586 |           }
587 |         }
588 |       }
589 |     }
590 |   }

Method code:
195 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
196 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
197 |     if (irNode.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
198 |       if (irNode.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
199 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
200 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
201 |       } else {
202 |         Node newBlock = newNode(Token.BLOCK, irNode);
203 |         newBlock.setLineno(irNode.getLineno());
204 |         newBlock.setCharno(irNode.getCharno());
205 |         irNode = newBlock;
206 |       }
207 |     }
208 |     return irNode;
209 |   }
