===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0559 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 867-889
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
867 |   private void updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
868 |     int i = 0;
869 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
870 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
871 |       JSType iParameterType = iParameter.getJSType();
872 |       if (iParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
873 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = (FunctionType) iParameterType;
874 | 
875 |         if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
876 |           // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
877 |           return;
878 |         }
879 |         Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
880 |         JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
881 |         if (iArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
882 |             iArgumentType instanceof FunctionType &&
883 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
884 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
885 |         }
886 |       }
887 |       i++;
888 |     }
889 |   }

===== M0567 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 776-790
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
776 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
777 |     if (item == null) {
778 |       return null;
779 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
780 |       return item.next;
781 |     } else {
782 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
783 |       if (result != item.next) {
784 |         return new PropListItem(
785 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
786 |       } else {
787 |         return item;
788 |       }
789 |     }
790 |   }

===== M0573 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 896-986
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
896 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
897 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
898 | 
899 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
900 |       return;
901 |     }
902 | 
903 |     int i = 0;
904 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
905 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
906 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
907 |       JSType iParameterType =
908 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
909 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
910 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
911 |         JSType iArgumentType = null;
912 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
913 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
914 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
915 |           if (!(iArgumentType instanceof ObjectType)) {
916 |             compiler.report(
917 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
918 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE));
919 |             return;
920 |           }
921 |         }
922 | 
923 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
924 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
925 |         int j = 0;
926 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
927 |           JSType jParameterType =
928 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
929 |           if (jParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
930 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = (FunctionType) jParameterType;
931 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
932 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
933 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
934 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
935 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
936 |                 return;
937 |               }
938 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
939 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
940 |               if (jArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
941 |                   jArgumentType instanceof FunctionType) {
942 |                 if (iArgumentType != null &&
943 |                     // null and undefined get filtered out above.
944 |                     !iArgumentType.isNoType()) {
945 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
946 |                   // using the actual type of T.
947 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = (FunctionType) jArgumentType;
948 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
949 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
950 |                     // function.
951 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
952 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
953 |                             jArgumentFnType, (ObjectType) iArgumentType));
954 |                   }
955 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal does not
956 |                   // reference this.
957 |                   if (!NodeUtil.referencesThis(
958 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
959 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
960 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNREAD_THIS));
961 |                   }
962 |                 } else {
963 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
964 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
965 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
966 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
967 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
968 |                   }
969 |                 }
970 |               }
971 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
972 |               // types of the arguments match.
973 |             }
974 |           }
975 |           j++;
976 |         }
977 | 
978 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
979 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
980 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
981 |           return;
982 |         }
983 |       }
984 |       i++;
985 |     }
986 |   }

/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1211 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1212 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1213 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1214 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1215 |       if (relationship != null) {
1216 |         ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
1217 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
1218 |         ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
1219 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
1220 |         if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
1221 |           FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1222 |           FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1223 | 
1224 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
1225 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1226 |           }
1227 | 
1228 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1229 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1230 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1231 |           }
1232 |         }
1233 |       }
1234 | 
1235 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1236 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1237 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1238 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1239 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1240 |         if (objectType != null) {
1241 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1242 | 
1243 |           if (functionType != null) {
1244 |             FunctionType getterType =
1245 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1246 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1247 |                 objectType);
1248 |           }
1249 |         }
1250 |       }
1251 |

1253 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);

1255 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);

1260 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1261 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1262 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1263 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1264 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1265 |         } else {
1266 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1267 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1268 |         }
1269 |       }
1270 |     }

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 693-729
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
693 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
694 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
695 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
696 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
697 | 
698 |     if (existingType != null) {
699 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
700 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
701 |         FunctionType existingFn =
702 |             isInstanceObject ?
703 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
704 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
705 | 
706 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
707 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
708 |         }
709 | 
710 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
711 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
712 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
713 |         }
714 | 
715 |         return existingFn;
716 |       } else {
717 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
718 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
719 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
720 |       }
721 |     }
722 | 
723 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
724 | 
725 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
726 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
727 |     }
728 |     return fnType;
729 |   }

===== M0603 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 370-383
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param msg An extra message for the mismatch warning, if necessary.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
370 |   boolean expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
371 |       JSType leftType, String msg) {
372 |     if (!rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
373 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
374 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
375 |         // the line numbers of both types.
376 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType);
377 |       } else {
378 |         mismatch(t, n, msg, rightType, leftType);
379 |       }
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 |     return true;
383 |   }

===== M0604 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 455-806
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
455 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
456 |     JSType childType;
457 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
458 |     Node left, right;
459 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
460 |     boolean typeable = true;
461 | 
462 |     switch (n.getType()) {
463 |       case Token.NAME:
464 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
465 |         break;
466 | 
467 |       case Token.LP:
468 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
469 |         // ignored here.
470 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
471 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
472 |         } else {
473 |           typeable = false;
474 |         }
475 |         break;
476 | 
477 |       case Token.COMMA:
478 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
479 |         break;
480 | 
481 |       case Token.TRUE:
482 |       case Token.FALSE:
483 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
484 |         break;
485 | 
486 |       case Token.THIS:
487 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
488 |         break;
489 | 
490 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
491 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
492 |         break;
493 | 
494 |       case Token.GET_REF:
495 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
496 |         break;
497 | 
498 |       case Token.NULL:
499 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
500 |         break;
501 | 
502 |       case Token.NUMBER:
503 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
504 |         break;
505 | 
506 |       case Token.STRING:
507 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
508 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
509 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
510 |         }
511 |         break;
512 | 
513 |       case Token.GET:
514 |       case Token.SET:
515 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
519 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
520 |         break;
521 | 
522 |       case Token.REGEXP:
523 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
524 |         break;
525 | 
526 |       case Token.GETPROP:
527 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
528 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
529 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.GETELEM:
533 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
534 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
535 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
536 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
537 |         typeable = false;
538 |         break;
539 | 
540 |       case Token.VAR:
541 |         visitVar(t, n);
542 |         typeable = false;
543 |         break;
544 | 
545 |       case Token.NEW:
546 |         visitNew(t, n);
547 |         typeable = true;
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.CALL:
551 |         visitCall(t, n);
552 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
553 |         break;
554 | 
555 |       case Token.RETURN:
556 |         visitReturn(t, n);
557 |         typeable = false;
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.DEC:
561 |       case Token.INC:
562 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
563 |         validator.expectNumber(
564 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
565 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
566 |         break;
567 | 
568 |       case Token.NOT:
569 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
570 |         break;
571 | 
572 |       case Token.VOID:
573 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
574 |         break;
575 | 
576 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
577 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
578 |         break;
579 | 
580 |       case Token.BITNOT:
581 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
582 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
583 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
584 |               childType.toString());
585 |         }
586 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
587 |         break;
588 | 
589 |       case Token.POS:
590 |       case Token.NEG:
591 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
592 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
593 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
594 |         break;
595 | 
596 |       case Token.EQ:
597 |       case Token.NE: {
598 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
599 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
600 | 
601 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
602 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
603 |         TernaryValue result =
604 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
605 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
606 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
607 |             result = result.not();
608 |           }
609 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
610 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
611 |         }
612 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
613 |         break;
614 |       }
615 | 
616 |       case Token.SHEQ:
617 |       case Token.SHNE: {
618 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
619 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
620 | 
621 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
622 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
623 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
624 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
625 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
626 |               rightType.toString());
627 |         }
628 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
629 |         break;
630 |       }
631 | 
632 |       case Token.LT:
633 |       case Token.LE:
634 |       case Token.GT:
635 |       case Token.GE:
636 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
637 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
638 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
639 |           validator.expectNumber(
640 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
641 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
642 |           validator.expectNumber(
643 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
644 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
645 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
646 |           // OK.
647 |         } else {
648 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
649 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
650 |           // should match a string context.
651 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
652 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
653 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
654 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
655 |           message = "right side of comparison";
656 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
657 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
658 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
659 |         }
660 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
661 |         break;
662 | 
663 |       case Token.IN:
664 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
665 |         right = n.getLastChild();
666 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
667 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
668 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
669 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
670 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
671 |         break;
672 | 
673 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
674 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
675 |         right = n.getLastChild();
676 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
677 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
678 | 
679 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
680 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
681 |         validator.expectActualObject(
682 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
683 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
684 |         break;
685 | 
686 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
687 |         visitAssign(t, n);
688 |         typeable = false;
689 |         break;
690 | 
691 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
694 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
702 |       case Token.LSH:
703 |       case Token.RSH:
704 |       case Token.URSH:
705 |       case Token.DIV:
706 |       case Token.MOD:
707 |       case Token.BITOR:
708 |       case Token.BITXOR:
709 |       case Token.BITAND:
710 |       case Token.SUB:
711 |       case Token.ADD:
712 |       case Token.MUL:
713 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.DELPROP:
717 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
718 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
719 |         }
720 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
721 |         break;
722 | 
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
725 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
726 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
727 |         typeable = false;
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       case Token.WITH: {
731 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
732 |         childType = getJSType(child);
733 |         validator.expectObject(
734 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
735 |         typeable = false;
736 |         break;
737 |       }
738 | 
739 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
740 |         visitFunction(t, n);
741 |         break;
742 | 
743 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
744 |       case Token.LABEL:
745 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
746 |       case Token.SWITCH:
747 |       case Token.BREAK:
748 |       case Token.CATCH:
749 |       case Token.TRY:
750 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
751 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
752 |       case Token.BLOCK:
753 |       case Token.EMPTY:
754 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
755 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
756 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
757 |       case Token.THROW:
758 |         typeable = false;
759 |         break;
760 | 
761 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
762 |       case Token.DO:
763 |       case Token.FOR:
764 |       case Token.IF:
765 |       case Token.WHILE:
766 |         typeable = false;
767 |         break;
768 | 
769 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
770 |       case Token.AND:
771 |       case Token.HOOK:
772 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
773 |       case Token.OR:
774 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
775 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
776 |         } else {
777 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
778 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
779 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
780 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
781 |           } else {
782 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
783 |           }
784 |         }
785 |         if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
786 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
787 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
788 |           }
789 |         }
790 |         break;
791 | 
792 |       default:
793 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
794 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
795 |         break;
796 |     }
797 | 
798 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
799 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
800 |

===== M0639 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1063-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1063 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1064 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1065 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1066 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1067 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1068 |     if (var != null) {
1069 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1070 |       if (varType != null) {
1071 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1072 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1073 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1074 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1075 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1076 |         } else {
1077 |           return varType;
1078 |         }
1079 |       }
1080 |     }
1081 | 
1082 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1083 |     if (objType != null) {
1084 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1085 |     }
1086 | 
1087 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1088 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1089 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1090 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1091 |       if (regType != null) {
1092 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1093 |       }
1094 |     }
1095 | 
1096 |     return propertyType;
1097 |   }

===== M0640 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 595-625
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
595 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
596 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
597 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
598 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
599 |     if (isOptional) {
600 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
601 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
602 |       // error can be reported.
603 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
604 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
605 |         emittedWarning = true;
606 |       }
607 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
608 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
609 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
610 |         emittedWarning = true;
611 |       }
612 |     } else {
613 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
614 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
615 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
616 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
617 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
618 |         } else {
619 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
620 |         }
621 |         emittedWarning = true;
622 |       }
623 |     }
624 |     return emittedWarning;
625 |   }

===== M0642 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

===== M0644 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 380-393
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
381 |       Node ret;
382 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
383 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
384 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
385 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
386 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
387 |       } else {
388 |         ret = transform(n);
389 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
390 |       }
391 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
392 |       return ret;
393 |     }

889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1685-1710
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1685 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1686 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1687 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1688 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1689 |       skipEOLs();
1690 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1691 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1692 |       skipEOLs();
1693 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1694 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1695 |       skipEOLs();
1696 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1697 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1698 |       String string = stream.getString();
1699 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1700 |         skipEOLs();
1701 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1702 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1703 |         return newStringNode(string);
1704 |       } else {
1705 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1706 |       }
1707 |     }
1708 | 
1709 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1710 |   }

===== M0648 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 423-455
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
423 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
424 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
425 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
426 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
427 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
428 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
429 |     }
430 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
431 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
432 |       thisType.setValidator(new ThisTypeValidator());
433 |     } else if (owner != null &&
434 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
435 |       // If the function is of the form:
436 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
437 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
438 |       // if it's of the form:
439 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
440 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
441 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
442 |       Var ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerTypeName);
443 |       JSType ownerType = ownerVar == null ? null : ownerVar.getType();
444 |       FunctionType ownerFnType = ownerType instanceof FunctionType ?
445 |           (FunctionType) ownerType : null;
446 |       ObjectType instType =
447 |           ownerFnType == null || ownerFnType.isOrdinaryFunction() ?
448 |           null : ownerFnType.getInstanceType();
449 |       if (instType != null) {
450 |         thisType = instType;
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     return this;
455 |   }

===== M0650 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1526-1561
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1526 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1527 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1528 | 
1529 |     skipEOLs();
1530 | 
1531 |     boolean restArg = false;
1532 |     token = next();
1533 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1534 |       token = next();
1535 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1536 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1537 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1538 |       }
1539 |       restArg = true;
1540 |     }
1541 | 
1542 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1543 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1544 |       skipEOLs();
1545 |       if (restArg) {
1546 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1547 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1548 |         next();
1549 |         skipEOLs();
1550 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1551 |       }
1552 | 
1553 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1554 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1555 |       } else {
1556 |         next();
1557 |       }
1558 |     }
1559 | 
1560 |     return typeNode;
1561 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0686 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 188-300
Documentation:

Method code:
188 |   @Override
189 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
190 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
191 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
192 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
193 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
194 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
195 |     // worthwhile.
196 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
197 |     Node condition = null;
198 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
199 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
200 | 
201 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
202 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
203 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
204 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
205 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
206 | 
207 |       switch (branch) {
208 |         case ON_TRUE:
209 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
210 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
211 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
212 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
213 | 
214 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
215 | 
216 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
217 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
218 |             }
219 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
220 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
221 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
222 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
223 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
224 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
225 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
226 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
227 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
228 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
229 |                 }
230 |               }
231 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(), iterKeyType);
232 |             }
233 |             newScope = informed;
234 |             break;
235 |           }
236 | 
237 |           // FALL THROUGH
238 | 
239 |         case ON_FALSE:
240 |           if (condition == null) {
241 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
242 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
243 |               condition = source;
244 | 
245 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
246 |               // of the loop.
247 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
248 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
249 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
250 |               }
251 |             }
252 |           }
253 | 
254 |           if (condition != null) {
255 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
256 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
257 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
258 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
259 |               // scope on false.
260 |               //
261 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
262 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
263 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
264 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
265 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
266 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
267 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
268 | 
269 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
270 |               // of the loop.
271 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
272 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
273 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
274 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
275 |               }
276 |               newScope =
277 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
278 |                       condition,
279 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
280 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
281 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
282 |             } else {
283 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
284 |               // of the loop.
285 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
286 |                 conditionFlowScope =
287 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
288 |               }
289 |               newScope =
290 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
291 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
292 |             }
293 |           }
294 |           break;
295 |       }
296 | 
297 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
298 |     }
299 |     return result;
300 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2287-2303
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2287 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2288 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2289 |     int c;
2290 |     while (true) {
2291 |       c = stream.getChar();
2292 |       if (c == ' ') {
2293 |         continue;
2294 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2295 |         matchedLc = true;
2296 |         break;
2297 |       } else {
2298 |         break;
2299 |       }
2300 |     }
2301 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2302 |     return matchedLc;
2303 |   }

===== M0695 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1924-1926
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1924 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1925 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1926 |   }

868 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
869 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
870 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
874 | 
875 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
876 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
877 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
878 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
879 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
880 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
881 |       JSType windowType = getType("Window");
882 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
883 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
884 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
885 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
886 |         } else {
887 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
888 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
889 |         }
890 |       }
891 |     }
892 |   }

===== M0717 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }

===== M0740 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 239-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
239 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
240 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
241 |     if (oldType == null) {
242 |       return this;
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
246 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
247 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
248 |       // Not a function literal.
249 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
250 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
251 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
252 |       }
253 |     } else {
254 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
255 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
256 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
257 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
258 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
259 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
260 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
261 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
262 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
263 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
264 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
265 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
266 | 
267 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
268 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
269 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
270 | 
271 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
272 |           // arguments.
273 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
274 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
275 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
276 |           }
277 |         } else {
278 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
279 |               paramBuilder,
280 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
281 |               warnedAboutArgList,
282 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
283 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
284 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
285 |         }
286 |       }
287 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
288 |     }
289 |     return this;
290 |   }

===== M0753 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()

/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1452 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1453 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1454 |         Node n = stub.node;
1455 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1456 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1457 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1458 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1459 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1460 | 
1461 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1462 |           continue;
1463 |         }
1464 | 
1465 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1466 |         // in the type registry.
1467 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1468 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1469 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1470 | 
1471 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1472 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1473 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1474 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1475 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1476 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern, n);
1477 |         } else {
1478 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1479 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1480 |         }
1481 |       }
1482 |     }

===== M0782 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 629-656
Documentation:

Method code:
629 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
630 |     Node lastJump = null;
631 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
632 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
633 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunction(curHandler)) {
634 |         break;
635 |       }
636 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
637 |         if (lastJump == null) {
638 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
639 |         } else {
640 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
641 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
642 |         }
643 |         lastJump = curHandler;
644 |       }
645 |     }
646 | 
647 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
648 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
649 |     }
650 | 
651 |     if (lastJump == null) {
652 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
653 |     } else {
654 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
655 |     }
656 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }

363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 349-396
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
349 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
350 |     if (info != null) {
351 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
352 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
353 | 
354 |       // base type
355 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
356 |         if (isConstructor) {
357 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
358 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
359 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
360 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
361 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
362 |           }
363 |         } else {
364 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
365 |         }
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       // implemented interfaces
369 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
370 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
371 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
372 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
373 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
374 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
375 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
376 |           }
377 |         }
378 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
379 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
380 |       }
381 | 
382 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
383 |       if (isInterface) {
384 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
385 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
386 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
387 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
388 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
389 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
390 |           }
391 |         }
392 |       }
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     return this;
396 |   }

===== M0825 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 120-170
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
121 |       // null is the smallest value
122 |       if (p2 == null) {
123 |         if (p1 == null) {
124 |           return 0;
125 |         } else {
126 |           return P1_GT_P2;
127 |         }
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       // check level
131 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
132 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
133 |       }
134 | 
135 |       // sourceName comparison
136 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
137 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
138 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
139 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
140 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
141 |           return sourceCompare;
142 |         }
143 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
144 |         return P1_LT_P2;
145 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
146 |         return P1_GT_P2;
147 |       }
148 |       // lineno comparison
149 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
150 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
151 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
152 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
153 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
154 |         return P1_LT_P2;
155 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
156 |         return P1_GT_P2;
157 |       }
158 |       // charno comparison
159 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
160 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
161 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
162 |         return charno1 - charno2;
163 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
164 |         return P1_LT_P2;
165 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
166 |         return P1_GT_P2;
167 |       }
168 |       // description
169 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
170 |     }

===== M0862 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 978-1023
Documentation:

Method code:
978 |   @Override
979 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
980 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this);
981 | 
982 |     call = (ArrowType) safeResolve(call, t, scope);
983 |     prototype = (FunctionPrototypeType) safeResolve(prototype, t, scope);
984 | 
985 |     // Warning about typeOfThis if it doesn't resolve to an ObjectType
986 |     // is handled further upstream.
987 |     //
988 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
989 |     //
990 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
991 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
992 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
993 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
994 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
995 |     // the name for them.
996 |     JSType maybeTypeOfThis = safeResolve(typeOfThis, t, scope);
997 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis != null) {
998 |       maybeTypeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
999 |     }
1000 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis instanceof ObjectType) {
1001 |       typeOfThis = (ObjectType) maybeTypeOfThis;
1002 |     }
1003 | 
1004 |     boolean changed = false;
1005 |     ImmutableList.Builder<ObjectType> resolvedInterfaces =
1006 |         ImmutableList.builder();
1007 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
1008 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
1009 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
1010 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
1011 |     }
1012 |     if (changed) {
1013 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
1014 |     }
1015 | 
1016 |     if (subTypes != null) {
1017 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
1018 |         subTypes.set(i, (FunctionType) subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope));
1019 |       }
1020 |     }
1021 | 
1022 |     return super.resolveInternal(t, scope);
1023 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)

Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

357 |   private void handleIf(Node node) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.toString()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 773-815
Documentation:
/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
773 |   @Override
774 |   public String toString() {
775 |     if (this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
776 |       return "Function";
777 |     }
778 | 
779 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
780 |     b.append("function (");
781 |     int paramNum = call.parameters.getChildCount();
782 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !typeOfThis.isUnknownType();
783 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
784 |       if (isConstructor()) {
785 |         b.append("new:");
786 |       } else {
787 |         b.append("this:");
788 |       }
789 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toString());
790 |     }
791 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
792 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
793 |         b.append(", ");
794 |       }
795 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
796 |       if (p.isVarArgs()) {
797 |         appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
798 |       } else {
799 |         b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
800 |       }
801 |       p = p.getNext();
802 |       while (p != null) {
803 |         b.append(", ");
804 |         if (p.isVarArgs()) {
805 |           appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
806 |         } else {
807 |           b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
808 |         }
809 |         p = p.getNext();
810 |       }
811 |     }
812 |     b.append("): ");
813 |     b.append(call.returnType);
814 |     return b.toString();
815 |   }

===== M0888 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 436-488
Documentation:

Method code:
436 |   @Override
437 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
438 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
439 |       return true;
440 |     }
441 | 
442 |     // Union types
443 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
444 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
445 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
446 |       return false;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // record types
450 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
451 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // Interfaces
455 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
456 |     // to the interface instance.
457 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
458 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
459 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
460 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
461 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
462 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
463 |           return true;
464 |         }
465 |       }
466 |     }
467 | 
468 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
469 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
470 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
471 |           return true;
472 |         }
473 |       }
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     // other prototype based objects
477 |     if (that != null) {
478 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
479 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
480 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
481 |         // to avoid guessing.
482 |         return true;
483 |       }
484 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     return false;
488 |   }
