===== M0898 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitReturn(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1629-1664
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a RETURN node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1629 |   private void visitReturn(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1630 |     Node function = t.getEnclosingFunction();
1631 | 
1632 |     // This is a misplaced return, but the real JS will fail to compile,
1633 |     // so let it go.
1634 |     if (function == null) {
1635 |       return;
1636 |     }
1637 |     JSType jsType = getJSType(function);
1638 | 
1639 |     if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
1640 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
1641 | 
1642 |       JSType returnType = functionType.getReturnType();
1643 | 
1644 |       // if no return type is specified, undefined must be returned
1645 |       // (it's a void function)
1646 |       if (returnType == null) {
1647 |         returnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1648 |       }
1649 | 
1650 |       // fetching the returned value's type
1651 |       Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
1652 |       JSType actualReturnType;
1653 |       if (valueNode == null) {
1654 |         actualReturnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1655 |         valueNode = n;
1656 |       } else {
1657 |         actualReturnType = getJSType(valueNode);
1658 |       }
1659 | 
1660 |       // verifying
1661 |       validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, valueNode, actualReturnType, returnType,
1662 |           "inconsistent return type");
1663 |     }
1664 |   }

===== M0922 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 817-861
Documentation:

Method code:
817 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
818 |       Node callNode) {
819 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
820 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
821 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
822 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
823 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
824 |       return scope;
825 |     }
826 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
827 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
828 |       return scope;
829 |     }
830 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
831 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
832 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
833 |     if (assertedType == null) {
834 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
835 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
836 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
837 |         if (type != narrowed) {
838 |           scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
839 |           redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
840 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
841 |         }
842 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
843 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
844 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
845 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
846 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
847 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
848 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
849 |       }
850 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
851 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
852 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
853 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
854 |       if (type != narrowed) {
855 |         scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
856 |         redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
857 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
858 |       }
859 |     }
860 |     return scope;
861 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 474-543
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
474 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
475 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
476 |     if (argsParent == null) {
477 |       if (info == null) {
478 |         return this;
479 |       } else {
480 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
481 |       }
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     // arguments
485 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
486 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
487 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
488 |     }
489 | 
490 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
491 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
492 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
493 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
494 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
495 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
496 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
497 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
498 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
499 | 
500 |       // type from JSDocInfo
501 |       JSType parameterType = null;
502 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
503 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
504 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
505 |         parameterType =
506 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
507 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
508 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
509 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
510 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
511 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
512 |       } else {
513 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
514 |       }
515 | 
516 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
517 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
518 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
519 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
520 |         }
521 |         foundTemplateType = true;
522 |       }
523 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
524 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
525 |           isOptionalParam,
526 |           isVarArgs);
527 | 
528 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
529 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
530 |       }
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
534 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
535 |     }
536 | 
537 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
538 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
539 |     }
540 | 
541 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
542 |     return this;
543 |   }

===== M0926 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 494-540
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
494 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
495 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
496 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
497 |     switch (left.getType()) {
498 |       case Token.NAME:
499 |         String varName = left.getString();
500 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
501 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
502 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
503 |         }
504 | 
505 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
506 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
507 |         // This has two purposes:
508 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
509 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
510 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
511 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
512 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
513 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
514 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
515 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
516 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
517 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
518 |         }
519 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
520 |             resultType : null);
521 | 
522 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
523 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
524 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
525 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
526 |         }
527 |         break;
528 |       case Token.GETPROP:
529 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
530 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
531 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
532 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
533 |               resultType);
534 |         }
535 | 
536 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
537 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
538 |         break;
539 |     }
540 |   }

===== M0927 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0928 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 84-106
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param inExterns {@code true} if this property was defined in an externs
   *        file. TightenTypes assumes that any function passed to an externs

255 |     // to register this after defining the property.
256 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
